</script><script type="text/javascript">
$(document).ready(function () {


    // Check the current page URL
    const path = window.location.pathname;

    // Check if the user has already clicked a button
    const hasClickedButton = localStorage.getItem('questionnaireClicked');

    // Conditionally show the Participate Now button
    if (hasClickedButton !== 'true' && 
        !path.includes('/corporate-training') && 
        !path.includes('/business-industry')) {
        $('#participateBtn').show(); // Show only if conditions are met
    }

    // Show the modal when the Participate button is clicked
    $('#participateBtn').on('click', function (e) {
        e.preventDefault(); // Prevent any default behavior
        $('#modalParticipate').fadeIn(); // Smooth fade-in effect
    });

    // Close the modal when the close button is clicked
    $('#closeBtn').on('click', function () {
        $('#modalParticipate').fadeOut(); // Smooth fade-out effect
    });

    // Close the modal when clicking outside the modal content
    $(window).on('click', function (e) {
        if ($(e.target).is('#modalParticipate')) {
            $('#modalParticipate').fadeOut();
        }
    });

    // Handle button clicks for Domestic and International
    $('.modal-button').on('click', function (e) {
        const url = $(this).data('url');
        window.open(url, '_blank'); // Open the URL in a new tab

        // Set a flag in localStorage indicating the user clicked a button
        localStorage.setItem('questionnaireClicked', 'true');

        // Hide the Participate Now button
        $('#participateBtn').fadeOut(); // Smooth disappearance
        $('#modalParticipate').fadeOut(); // Smooth disappearance of the modal
    });

    // Optional: Prevent unintended page reload when buttons are clicked
    $('.modal-button').on('click', function (e) {
        e.preventDefault(); // Prevent default action for safety
        console.log(`Button clicked: ${$(this).text()}`); // Log button click
    });
});
</script>

      <div class="right content">
        <h1>International Students Tuition and Fees</h1>




<h2>How much will my first year of studies cost?</h2>

<p>The following estimates provide a cost breakdown of tuition, fees and approximate living costs for an international student at NAIT for 2 semesters of study (8 months).</p>

<h3>Total NAIT and living costs: $45,606 CAD*</h3>




<div class="row " id=""><div class="col half  ">

<img src="/getmedia/911c0a04-af56-4e20-89d1-22510871b2cb/Recruitment_Web_TuitionInfographicsUpdate_20260630_INTL.png.aspx?width=1400&height=585&ext=.png&maxsidesize=50" data-src="/getmedia/911c0a04-af56-4e20-89d1-22510871b2cb/Recruitment_Web_TuitionInfographicsUpdate_20260630_INTL.png.aspx?width=1400&height=585&ext=.png&maxsidesize=1000" alt="circular infographic displaying NAIT costs including tuition, transit pass and books and supplies." class="image-widget lazyload " />
</div><div class="col half ">

<img src="/getmedia/10d5ea2b-9965-4a45-b1d1-138c53d2bd67/Recruitment_Web_TuitionInfographicsUpdate_20260630_Domestic2.png.aspx?width=1400&height=585&ext=.png&maxsidesize=50" data-src="/getmedia/10d5ea2b-9965-4a45-b1d1-138c53d2bd67/Recruitment_Web_TuitionInfographicsUpdate_20260630_Domestic2.png.aspx?width=1400&height=585&ext=.png&maxsidesize=1000" alt="Circular infographic displaying estimated living costs including food, rent and clothing expenses." class="image-widget lazyload " />
</div></div><div class="grey box"><p>*This information is a&nbsp;2026/27 estimated average of student costs for one year.&nbsp;Books and supplies are not included in tuition and must be paid for separately.&nbsp;This information will be updated periodically and may change without notice. Check your program&rsquo;s page for a more detailed estimate of tuition, fees, and books and supplies. All fees are subject to change.</p>
</div>

		<h2  id="">International tuition by program</h2>
	<p>The following program fees apply to <strong>international students</strong> who are enrolled in the listed&nbsp;<strong>academic year</strong>. Fees are charged per term. Tuition and fees per term consist of: (1) tuition and (2) mandatory fees.</p>

<ul>
	<li>View the tuition rate for each program in the table below.</li>
	<li>For information on mandatory fees &ndash; including program and material fees, recreation and athletic fees, and NAITSA fees &ndash; refer to the list of&nbsp;<a class="external-link" href="https://www.nait.ca/nait/admissions/financial-planning/tuition-and-fees/tuition-and-additional-fees/additional-fees-and-expenses">additional fees and expenses</a>.</li>
	<li>Students who are protected persons or&nbsp;convention refugee in Canada<strong> </strong>may be eligible to have their tuition and fees assessed at the domestic rate. <a href="https://www.nait.ca/nait/international/immigration-visa-requirements/emergency-supports-for-international-students" target="_self">Learn more about eligibility and how to apply.</a></li>
</ul>






<script type="text/javascript">

//alert("wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27");

    $(document).ready(function () {
            var tabText = $("#walkthrough-tabs-nav-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27 > li:first-child a").text();
            $("#tabs-mobile-dropdown-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27 > .dropdown-text").text(tabText);

            if ( $(window).width() > 768 ){
              
                $(document).tooltip({
                  track: true,
                  show: {
                      delay: 1000,
                  }
                });
             }

         
              var tabsCount = $("#tabs-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27 >ul >li").length;
              var pbarStep = 100 / tabsCount;
              var Percentagebar = pbarStep;
              var orientation = "top";
              var displaypbar = "False";
              $("#progressbar-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27").progressbar({ value: Percentagebar });
              $("#prevBtn-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27").bind("click", prevOfferTab);
              $("#nextBtn-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27").bind("click", nextOfferTab);
              $("#prevBtn-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27").hide();


              // Change the orientation of tabs
              if (orientation == "left") {
                  $("#tabs-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27").tabs().addClass("ui-tabs-vertical ui-helper-clearfix vertical-tabs");
                  $("#tabs-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27 li").removeClass("ui-corner-top").addClass("ui-corner-left");
              }

              if ( $(window).width() > 950 ){  
                var mobileWidth = true;
              } else { 
                var mobileWidth = false;
              }
            
              $("#tabs-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27").tabs({
                    
                      overflowTabs: mobileWidth,
                    
                      activate: function (event, ui) {
                        var active = $('#tabs-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27').tabs('option', 'active');
                        var step = active + 1;
                        var tabsCount = $("#tabs-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27 >ul >li").length;

                        if (active == 0) {
                          $("#prevBtn-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27").hide();
                           Percentagebar = pbarStep;
                        
                        }
                        else {
                          $("#prevBtn-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27").show();
                          Percentagebar = step * pbarStep;
                        }

                        var tabsCount = $("#tabs-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27 >ul >li").length - 1;

                        if (active == tabsCount) {
                          $("#nextBtn-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27").hide();
                          $("#prevBtn-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27").show();
                        }
                        else {
                          $("#nextBtn-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27").show();
                        }
               
                        $("#progressbar-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27").progressbar({ value: Percentagebar });

                        //window.location.hash = ui.newPanel.attr('id');
                        if(history.pushState) {
                            window.history.pushState('','', '#' + ui.newPanel.attr('id'));
                        }else {
                            location.hash = ui.newPanel.attr('id');
                        }
                     
                  }
          
               });

          function getSelectedTabIndex(change) {
              var $tabs = $('#tabs-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27').tabs();
              var selected = $tabs.tabs('option', 'active') + change;

              if (selected == 0) {
                  $("#prevBtn-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27").hide();
              }
              else {
                  $("#prevBtn-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27").show();
              }

              var tabsCount = $("#tabs-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27 >ul >li").length - 1;
              
              if (selected == tabsCount) {
                  $("#nextBtn-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27").hide();
              }
              else {
                  $("#nextBtn-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27").show();
              }

              return selected;
          }

          function nextOfferTab() {
             
              var newTabIndex = parseInt(getSelectedTabIndex(1));
              $('#tabs-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27').tabs('option', 'active', newTabIndex);
              $("#progressbar-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27").progressbar({ value: Percentagebar });

              var active = $('#tabs-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27').tabs('option', 'active');
              var tabsCount = $("#tabs-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27 >ul >li").length;

              var tabText = $("#walkthrough-tabs-nav-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27 li a").eq(active).text();

              for (var i = 0; i <= tabsCount; i++) {
                  $("#li-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27" + i).removeClass('ui-tabs-previous');
              }

                  for (var i = 0; i <= active; i++) {
                      $("#li-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27" + i).addClass('ui-tabs-previous');
                  }
               $("#tabs-mobile-dropdown-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27 > .dropdown-text").text(tabText);

          }

          function prevOfferTab() {
           
              var newTabIndex = parseInt(getSelectedTabIndex(-1));
              $('#tabs-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27').tabs('option', 'active', newTabIndex);
              $("#progressbar-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27").progressbar({ value: Percentagebar });

              var active = $('#tabs-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27').tabs('option', 'active');
              var tabsCount = $("#tabs-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27 >ul >li").length;
              var tabText = $("#walkthrough-tabs-nav-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27 li a").eq(active).text();

              for (var i = 0; i <= tabsCount; i++) {
                 
                  $("#li-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27" + i).removeClass('ui-tabs-previous');
                  }

                  for (var i = 0; i <= active; i++) {
                     
                      $("#li-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27" + i).addClass('ui-tabs-previous');
                  }
                $("#tabs-mobile-dropdown-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27 > .dropdown-text").text(tabText);
          }
      
          $('#walkthrough-tabs-nav-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27 li a').click(function () {
              var text = $(this).text();
              var active = $('#tabs-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27').tabs('option', 'active');
              var tabsCount = $("#tabs-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27 >ul >li").length;

              for (var i = 0; i <= tabsCount; i++) {
                 
                  $("#li-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27" + i).removeClass('ui-tabs-previous');
                }

              for (var i = 0; i <= active; i++) {
                  
                  $("#li-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27" + i).addClass('ui-tabs-previous');
              }
              $("#tabs-mobile-dropdown-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27 > .dropdown-text").text(text);
              
          });

      });

      $(document).ready(function () {

          $('#tabs-mobile-dropdown-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27').click(function (e) {
              e.preventDefault();
              $(this).toggleClass('active');
              $(this).next('.ui-tabs-nav').toggleClass('show');

              $('#walkthrough-tabs-nav-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27 li').click(function (){
                $('#walkthrough-tabs-nav-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27').removeClass('show');
                $('#tabs-mobile-dropdown-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a27').removeClass('active');
                
              });


          });
          
      });    


</script>

</div><div id="tabs-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a274" class="widget-content"><p>For international students enrolled in continuing education courses or programs,&nbsp; tuition and fees are determined on an individual basis. Some continuing education programs may be eligible for an international study permit, based on government criteria. Learn more about taking <a href="/coned/english-as-a-second-language" target="_self">ESL at NAIT</a> or <a href="/coned/academic-upgrading" target="_self">Academic Upgrading at NAIT</a>.</p>




</div><div id="tabs-wp_0fccaf47761746deb672cdeaeb8f2a275" class="widget-content"><h2>Open Studies</h2>

<p>The costs for Open Studies courses are made up of two types of fees: tuition and mandatory fees. You can&nbsp;refer to the&nbsp;<a class="local-link" href="https://www.nait.ca/nait/admissions/financial-planning/additional-fees-and-expenses" target="_self">Additional Fees and Expenses section</a>&nbsp;for details on mandatory fees. Please note&nbsp;that U-Pass and&nbsp;Health and Dental benefits are mandatory fees for students enrolled in a single credit course at NAIT.&nbsp;</p>

<h3>Tuition Fees</h3>

<p>Tuition for Open Studies is based on the credit value of each course you are enrolled in. Rates are subject to change each academic year. The 2025/26&nbsp;academic year rate&nbsp;below is effective from July 1, 2025&nbsp;to August 31, 2026; then the 2026/27&nbsp;rates commence.</p>

<div id="foipModal">
<h3>Important Notice</h3>

<p>On June 11, 2025, the Government of Alberta repealed the Alberta&rsquo;s <i>Freedom of Information and Protection of Privacy Act</i> (FOIP) and replaced it with the following two new pieces of legislation:</p>

<ul>
	<li><strong><a href="https://kings-printer.alberta.ca/1266.cfm?page=a01p4.cfm&amp;leg_type=Acts&amp;isbncln=9780779851331" target="_blank">Access to Information Act (ATIA)</a></strong>: ATIA establishes rights for persons to access records, as defined in the ATIA, that are in the custody or control of public bodies subject to limited and specific exceptions.<!--
	</li--></li>
	<li><strong><a href="https://kings-printer.alberta.ca/1266.cfm?page=p28p5.cfm&amp;leg_type=Acts&amp;isbncln=9780779851348" target="_blank">Protection of Privacy Act (POPA)</a></strong>: POPA establishes privacy rights for Albertans concerning personal information. It also permits public bodies to collect, use or disclose personal information in new ways, including for data matching and to create non-personal data.</li>
</ul>

<p>NAIT&rsquo;s various webpages dealing with FOIP are currently being updated to incorporate the recent changes in legislation.</p>

<p>Thank you for your patience as NAIT updates all content to comply with the new legislation.</p>
</div>
</div>
